### 2026-09-08 21:22:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #Angola #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Angola inflation Rate Keeps Cooling — The annual inflation rate in Angola fell further to 8.78% in August 2026 from 9.33% in July, marking the lowest since April 2015 and extending the downward trend observed since mid-2024. The moderation in inflation has been attributed mainly to the relative stability of the kwanza and improvements in the domestic supply of essential goods. Price growth eased for several CPI categories, including food & non-alcoholic beverages (9.94% vs 10.40% in July); clothing & footwear (6.20% vs 6.30%); housing & utilities (8.24% vs 10.17%); miscellaneous goods & services (6.02% vs 6.38%) and education (19.33% vs 25.24%). Meanwhile, prices increased slightly faster for alcoholic beverages & tobacco (9.02% vs 8.61%) and transportation (3.67% vs 3.65%). On a monthly basis, the CPI rose by 0.59%, slowing from a 0.75% increase in the prior month.

### 2026-09-08 21:14:11  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Lumber Hits Nine-Month Low — Lumber futures fell below $555 per thousand board feet, reaching its lowest level in nine months, as retaliatory tariffs from Canada on US goods took effect, weighing down demand. Ottawa had announced counter-tariffs of 25% on US lumber and 50% on Plywood following US tariffs on several lumber-related goods. US forestry groups have long accused Canada of distorting the market, as 94% of Canadian forestland is publicly owned compared to 58% privately owned in the US, allowing Canadian lumber to be sold at a lower price. The fallout is hitting the forest sector of both countries, as selling lumber prices went up immediately after the news, pushing demand lower. Fall is usually a time for renovations, however, elevated prices weighs on the sector. Meanwhile, US housing remains subdued, with lumber-intensive single-family construction spending falling 3.2% in July, while elevated mortgage rates continue to weigh on homeownership.

---

### 2026-09-08 21:14:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Used Car Prices MoM #Importance 1

US Used-Car Prices Fall for Second Month — The Manheim Used Vehicle Value Index, a widely followed gauge of wholesale used-vehicle prices in the US, fell 0.9% month-on-month in August 2026, marking a second consecutive monthly decline. On a yearly basis, price growth slowed to 0.4%, the weakest pace since December 2025, as resilience in EV and compact-car prices was offset by continued declines in pickups and SUVs. Cox Automotive Chief Economist Jeremy Robb said depreciation was stronger in July and early August before easing toward the end of the month. While younger vehicle cohorts saw greater depreciation, older and more affordable cars held up better, with nine- and 10-year-old vehicles remaining about six percentage points above their long-term averages, underscoring steady demand for affordable used cars. Used retail sales continued to strengthen through August, while retail days’ supply fell to 44 days from 46 at the end of July. Stronger demand and tighter supply continued to support dealer purchases from Manheim.

### 2026-09-08 21:09:30  #市場

【全國豬肉價格連續4週上漲 豬肉價格上漲原因找到了】近期，在北京批發市場走訪瞭解到，受需求增長拉動，豬肉價格溫和回升。資料顯示，9月8日，北京新發地市場白條豬批發平均價為每公斤14元，比8月1日上漲12%。據介紹，豬肉價格上漲，一方面是因為天氣逐漸轉涼、肉類消費增加，另外，能繁母豬存欄量的下降也帶來了豬價上漲的預期。北京新發地市場分析師劉通判斷，從總體上來看，回升的態勢比較溫和，由於毛豬的存欄量仍然比較充足，所以後期肉價的漲勢也不會太大。從全國來看，全國豬肉價格和生豬價格也是呈現回升走勢。據農業農村部監測，9月份第1周全國生豬價格為每公斤11.59元，環比上漲0.3%，豬肉價格為每公斤21.23元，環比上漲0.2%，生豬價格和豬肉價格均是連續4週上漲。（央視財經）

### 2026-09-08 21:03:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

Swiss Bond Yield Holds Near One-Month High — Switzerland's 10-year government bond yield traded near 0.42%, remaining near the highest level since late July, amid inflation concerns. Swiss inflation doubled in August as tensions in the Middle East persisted over the month, increasing inflation concerns amid higher global energy prices, although its effects are expected to be temporary as electricity prices will drop around 4% starting next year, weighing on inflation in the following months. Meanwhile, quarterly economic growth was confirmed at 1.5%, its highest level in nearly five years, as a weaker swiss franc supported exports and helped lift the trade surplus to a record high, while a trade deal removing all tariffs on goods bound for China also provides support. On the monetary policy front, a Swiss Bankers Association survey showed that all bankers expect the SNB to keep its policy rate at 0% by year-end. Markets anticipated the first rate hike in June 2027, while most economists expect the first hike in early 2028.

【重慶市委書記袁家軍、市長陳新武會見聯想集團董事長楊元慶一行】據“重慶釋出”，9月8日下午，市委書記袁家軍，市委副書記、市長陳新武會見了聯想集團董事長兼CEO楊元慶一行。聯想集團執行副總裁、中國區總裁劉軍，副總裁戴京彤、楊青、王凌雲，市領導劉尚進、鄭向東參加。袁家軍代表市委、市政府歡迎楊元慶一行來渝深化合作，感謝聯想集團對重慶經濟社會發展的支援。袁家軍說，習近平總書記來渝視察時，賦予重慶奮力打造新時代西部大開發重要戰略支點、內陸開放綜合樞紐“兩大定位”，標定了重慶在中國式現代化發展全域性中的奮鬥座標。當前，全市上下深化落實習近平總書記重要講話重要指示精神，縱深推進國家重大戰略走深走實，做大做強“33618”現代製造業叢集體系，加快打造人工智慧創新應用高地，持續營造一流產業生態，紮實推動“十五五”開好局起好步。希望聯想集團繼續深耕重慶，不斷深化高階製造、人工智慧發展等方面務實合作，努力實現更高水平互利共贏。楊元慶感謝重慶市長期以來對聯想集團發展的關心支援，並對現代化新重慶建設取得的成績表示祝賀。他說，重慶工業基礎堅實，智慧製造領域積累了多年經驗，是聯想集團在中國重要的製造基地。集團將進一步堅定在渝發展信心，深度融入重慶產業體系和創新體系，積極發揮自身在智慧製造、AI應用領域的經驗優勢，更好賦能重慶相關產業智慧化轉型，助力重慶高質量發展。聯想集團，有關區和市有關部門負責人參加。

### 2026-09-08 20:57:55  #Trading Economics #Markets #Switzerland #Currency #Importance 0

Swiss Franc Steady Near Two-Week Low — The Swiss franc traded near $0.81, its lowest level since August 19th, amid inflation concerns. Swiss inflation doubled in August as tensions in the Middle East persisted over the month, increasing inflation concerns amid higher global energy prices, although its effects are expected to be temporary as electricity prices will drop around 4% starting next year, weighing on inflation in the following months. Meanwhile, quarterly economic growth was confirmed at 1.5%, its highest level in nearly five years, as a weaker swiss franc supported exports and helped lift the trade surplus to a record high, while a trade deal removing all tariffs on goods bound for China also provides support. On the monetary policy front, a Swiss Bankers Association survey showed that all bankers expect the SNB to keep its policy rate at 0% by year-end. Markets anticipated the first rate hike in June 2027, while most economists expect the first hike in early 2028.

【糖業股大漲，上市公司回應】9月8日，糖產業指數大漲5.94%，粵桂股份、中糧糖業漲停。訊息面上，據多家媒體報道，全球第二產糖國泰國的糖產量在新榨季可能至少下降17%，全球糖供應面臨的壓力或將進一步加大。某糖產業頭部上市公司人士回應稱，北半球今年的榨季還沒有開始，泰國和我國甘蔗榨季時間基本一致，因此目前還不好做出判斷。“一般而言，甘蔗榨季是從當年11月份到第二年的2月份、3月份，榨好的糖第二年銷售。”粵桂股份方面回應稱。近期糖價有一定的反彈，但前期跌了很多。當前屬於糖企當年銷售的尾聲。“食糖從原材料角度看，北方原材料主要是甜菜，主要分佈在東北、新疆等地。南方原材料主要是甘蔗，廣東、廣西、雲南等地都有種植。國內食糖基本為自產自銷，且需要進口一部分食糖。國內糖價會受到國際糖價的影響，但價格並不完全同步。”有上市公司高管表示。（中證金牛座）

### 2026-09-08 20:54:40  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Currency #Importance 1

Canadian Dollar Strengthens — The Canadian dollar strengthened to around 1.38 per USD in September, largely reflecting broad US dollar weakness. Meanwhile, Canada’s retaliatory tariffs on US goods took effect after Prime Minister Mark Carney’s government failed to reach a deal with Washington last month. The counter-tariffs cover $20 billion of US goods, with duties ranging from 15% to 50% on products including steel, furniture, clothing and electronics. The tariffs imposed last month targeted $20 billion, or 5%, of Canadian exports to the US, including wine, furniture, dairy products, cement, clothing, fishing rods and hockey equipment. Firmer crude prices, which typically support the loonie given Canada’s status as a major oil exporter, have provided an additional tailwind. Further upside risks to oil prices remain as Iran threatens to strike regional energy infrastructure in response to US actions. Energy-driven inflation concerns could also lead to tighter BoC policy.

【中國天眼構建世界最大中性氫星系樣本庫】記者從中國科學院國家天文臺獲悉，由國家天文臺姜鵬團隊牽頭的中國天眼FAST中性氫巡天專案（FASHI），釋出第二期資料集FASHI DR2，構建起當今世界規模最大的中性氫星系樣本庫，相關成果9月8日發表在學術期刊。中性氫是宇宙古老的基礎物質，被視作星系的“建築原料”，它發出的21釐米輻射訊號，能夠穿透星際塵埃，傳遞宇宙深處資訊。此次釋出的資料集共捕捉到15.6萬個中性氫星系，巡天覆蓋近半個天穹。樣本數量是此前國際巡天望遠鏡的5倍，相較首期資料的近4倍，再次重新整理世界紀錄。（新華社）

Silver Steadies Near $66 Ahead of US Inflation Data — Silver traded cautiously around $66 an ounce on Tuesday as investors weighed expectations of a Federal Reserve rate hike against a weaker dollar ahead of key US inflation data later this week. Rising oil prices also renewed concerns over inflation. Oil extended gains to multi-week highs amid escalating Middle East tensions after Yemen’s Iran-backed Houthis attacked energy facilities and cities in Saudi Arabia, a key US ally. Meanwhile, Friday’s stronger-than-expected jobs report pointed to a strengthening labor market, prompting traders to price in roughly a 60% chance of a Fed rate hike at the September meeting. While silver is traditionally seen as an inflation hedge, higher interest rates tend to reduce the appeal of the non-yielding asset. Investors now await producer price data on Thursday and consumer inflation figures on Friday for further clues on the Fed’s policy outlook.

【交易商協會對東方金誠予以警告 涉違反評級獨立性要求等】交易商協會公告，經查，東方金誠國際信用評估有限公司（以下簡稱“東方金誠”）作為銀行間債券市場信用評級機構，存在以下違反銀行間債券市場相關自律管理規定的行為：一是違反評級獨立性要求，營銷人員在某專案中多次參與發行人為獲取高信用級別而組織的資產整合商議，所籌劃的資產整合方案明確影響了評級結果；二是部分模型指標打分理由相互矛盾，不充分、不客觀；三是未嚴格執行內控制度中關於營銷制度、客戶信用風險監控處置的要求，合規監督機制落實不到位；四是盡職調查訪談混淆評級物件，部分記錄錯誤、相互套用，對部分評級指標未按自律規則要求充分蒐集必要資訊。根據銀行間債券市場相關自律規定，經自律處分會議審議決定，對東方金誠予以警告；責令其針對本次事件中暴露出的營銷合規管理、盡職調查等方面問題進行全面深入的整改。

TSX Futures Slip on US-Canada Trade War — Futures tracking Canada’s stock market slipped on Tuesday as Canada’s retaliatory tariffs on US goods took effect. The counter-tariffs cover $20 billion of US goods, with duties ranging from 15% to 50% on products including steel, furniture, clothing and electronics. The US tariffs implemented last month targeted sectors including wine, furniture, dairy products, cement, clothing, fishing rods and hockey equipment, covering $20 billion, or 5%, of Canadian exports to the US. Meanwhile, US President Trump said on Monday that Bombardier would not be allowed to sell its planes in the US unless it started manufacturing in the country. Also weighing on the TSX, crude prices climbed after Saudi Arabia reported attacks that halted operations at several energy facilities. Fears of prolonged energy disruptions and resulting energy-driven inflation increased rate hike expectations in Canada and the US, pressuring credit-sensitive stocks such as major banks.

US Futures Pull Back — US equity futures were firmly lower on Tuesday after a long weekend, as higher energy prices dented the outlook on margins and lifted risks of higher interest rates. Contracts for the S&P 500 and Nasdaq 100 were 0.5% down, and those for the Dow fell nearly 1%. New strikes between the US and Iran prolonged expectations on suspended fuel exports from the Persian Gulf, while attacks from Houthi forces damaged Saudi energy infrastructure and dented their capacity. Borrowing costs for US companies were also lifted by hawkish signs from the Bank of Japan and likely selling of US Treasury securities by Japan to support the yen. Credit-sensitive hyperscalers were firmly lower, with Microsoft and Alphabet down 1%, while Amazon fell 1% ahead of its sterling bond offer. AI companies have increasingly borrowed in foreign currencies as dollar bond markets already face soaring AI-debt supply. In turn, Oracle gained 4.5% ahead of its earnings this week.

### 2026-09-08 19:12:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Chile Annual Inflation Accelerates — Chile’s annual inflation rate accelerated to 4.1% in August 2026 from 3.5% in the previous month. The acceleration was mainly driven by higher price growth for food, which increased to 4.6% from 4.0%, and transport, which accelerated sharply to 6.8% from 4.6%. Inflation for recreation, sport, and culture also picked up, rising to 6.4% from 4.4%. Meanwhile, inflation stood at 3.4% for housing and utilities, at 6.6% for restaurants and lodging and 5.9% for education, while deflation in clothing and footwear eased to 4.0% from 4.8%. In contrast, the decline in prices for insurance and financial services accelerated to 3.6% from 3.4%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.6%, following a 0.1% increase in July. Core consumer prices, which excludes food and energy, rose 0.4% over the month, unchanged from July.

US 10-Year Treasury Yield Highest Since 2023 — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note topped 4.8% on Tuesday, the highest since October 2023, as traders returned from a long weekend to a week packed with key economic data that could help shape expectations for the Fed’s next moves. The CPI and PPI reports due this week are expected to provide further clues on inflationary pressures, particularly those linked to rising energy costs. Headline inflation is expected to remain steady at 3.4%, while the core rate is forecast to slow slightly to 2.4%. Meanwhile, both headline and core producer prices are expected to accelerate, reaching 5.3% and 4.6%, respectively. Oil prices have also continued to rise amid ongoing strikes in the Middle East, exacerbating concerns over supply disruptions. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 58% chance of a 25bps rate hike by the Fed next week. Meanwhile, Tuesday’s three-year Treasury auction kicks off a busy stretch of government debt issuance.

Albania Inflation Eases in August — The annual inflation rate in Albania fell to 3.2% in August 2026 from 3.4% in the previous month. Prices eased for food and non-alcoholic beverages (2.8% vs 3.5% in July), housing & utilities (3.7% vs 4.1%), furnishings & household equipment (2.8% vs 3.3%), miscellaneous goods & services (2.8% vs 3.1%), alcoholic beverages & tobacco (2.4% vs 3%), health (1.8% vs 1.9%), recreation & culture (5.4% vs 6%), and restaurants & hotels (2.3% vs 2.7%). Also, cost fell further for clothing & footwear (-3.2% vs -1.8%) and communications (-1% vs -0.9%). On the other hand, prices accelerated for transport (12% vs 8%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices went up 0.1%, after a 0.2% gain in July.

Sensex Closes at Near 3-Month Low — India’s BSE Sensex finished about 0.7% down at 75,577.6 on Tuesday, a level last seen in June and marking the second session of losses. The ongoing rally in oil prices and escalating hostilities in the Middle East continued to weigh on market sentiment, while concerns persisted over volatility stemming from the new closing-auction pricing mechanism. Banking heavyweights were among the key drags, led by a 2% decline in ICICI Bank, while Axis Bank and HDFC Bank fell 1.7% and 1.1%, respectively. Reliance Industries and UltraTech Cement declined 1.2% each. On the upside, Indian defense companies such as BEL (1.5%) rose after the government gave an initial approval for about $11 billion of proposed military purchases.

South African Rand Weakens Slightly — The South African rand edged down to around 16.1 per USD, a near one-week low, as traders assessed worse-than-expected domestic GDP data, while Middle East tensions kept risk sentiment subdued. South Africa’s GDP shrank by 0.2% in Q2, ending six quarters of expansion, as the escalating Middle East conflict disrupted energy-intensive mining and manufacturing sectors. Against this backdrop, the central bank (SARB) faces a delicate policy balancing act, with the need to bring inflation back to target while supporting economic growth. Persistent high energy prices continued to fuel inflation concerns and the prospect of tighter monetary policies globally. SARB Governor Lesetja Kganyago recently signaled that policymakers can afford to respond cautiously to the latest inflation shocks while remaining committed to the 3% target. Inflation eased to 4.3% in July from 5% in June, but the moderation could prove temporary due to fuel price adjustments in August and September.

【JP Morgan: SpaceX pushing into mobile telecoms — when could it threaten US Big Three?】JP Morgan says SpaceX signaled on its Q2 call that it plans to lean more on in‑house infrastructure — satellite capacity, femtocells integrated into Starlink broadband terminals and owned ground stations — potentially reducing reliance on traditional macro sites and fiber backhaul. JP Morgan expects SpaceX still cannot fully bypass large towers and low‑band spectrum; it projects SpaceX will begin building an independent US terrestrial network from 2028 and will ultimately need roughly 75,000 large towers. Meaningful competitive pressure on the three major US carriers is not expected to materialize in the next few years. (JP Morgan report, Sept. 4)

【委內瑞拉民眾對與特朗普的石油協議看法分裂 半數認為美國獲益更多】委內瑞拉民眾對於特朗普政府與該國臨時當局達成的石油協議意見嚴重分化，半數受訪者認為，這項協議將令美國比委內瑞拉獲益更多。民調顯示約38%的受訪者反對這項協議，約35%表示支援，另有27%表示不確定。調查結果凸顯出委內瑞拉代理總統德爾西·羅德里格斯面臨的政治挑戰。自美國總統特朗普宣佈美國將控制委內瑞拉逾650億桶原油儲量以來，羅德里格斯既受到執政黨強硬派的反對，也面臨反對派的批評。許多委內瑞拉人質疑協議是否公平，以及羅德里格斯是否有權進行相關談判。與此同時，她正試圖應對由此引發的政治反彈，並維持其執政地位。約六成受訪者預計，這項石油協議對其日常生活的影響將是“非常正面”“較為正面”或“中性”。但50%的人認為美國將從協議中獲益更多，28%認為兩國將同等受益，只有10%認為委內瑞拉獲益更多。

### 2026-09-08 18:12:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #NFIB Business Optimism Index #Importance 2

US Small Business Sentiments Deteriorates — The US NFIB Small Business Optimism Index fell to 98.7 in August 2026 from 99.8 in July and below forecasts of 99.3. Actual sales worsened, with a net negative 9% of owners reporting higher nominal sales in the past three months, the lowest since November 2025. Reports of inflation as the single most important problem increased to 16%, now tying with taxes as the second top small business issue. Reports of “labor quality or availability” and “labor costs” as owners’ single most important problem eased, with “labor costs” dropping to its lowest level since March 2021. In addition, expectations for better business conditions deteriorated. “Uncertainty remains elevated among small business owners as they face a mixed set of challenges with weakened sales, supply chain disruptions, and inflation pressures. While expectations for the overall economy dimmed, Main Street owners remain largely positive in the health of their own businesses”, said NFIB Chief Economist Bill Dunkelberg

### 2026-09-08 18:09:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Latvia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Latvia Annual Inflation Accelerates — Latvia’s annual inflation rate accelerated to 3.2% in August 2026 from 2.6% in the previous month. The acceleration was mainly due to higher price growth for transports, which rose sharply to 8.6% from 4.8% in July, as well as recreation, sports and culture (6.5% vs 4.8%), restaurants and hotels (7.6% vs 6.9%), health (4.2% vs 3.8%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (2.8% vs 1.1%) and insurance and financial services (6.8% vs 5.9%). Meanwhile, inflation for housing and utilities remained unchanged at 6.8%. In contrast, food prices continued to decline, falling sharply by 4% compared with a 3.1% drop in the prior month, while inflation moderated for clothing and footwear (0.7% vs 1.1%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices edged up 0.3% following a 0.7% decline in July.

【歐洲天然氣價格升至三年高點 交易商爭相補充庫存】歐洲天然氣價格升至三年新高。隨著冬季臨近，交易商爭相補充天然氣庫存，同時等待伊朗與阿曼就霍爾木茲海峽航運達成協議的具體細節。歐洲基準天然氣期貨週二一度上漲3.4%，升至2023年1月以來最高水平。儘管德黑蘭表示與阿曼協議即將達成，其中將包括一條臨時安全航線，但美國週末襲擊伊朗油輪後將作何反應，目前對交易員而言仍不明朗，船隻遇襲風險依然很高。沙烏地阿拉伯能源設施遭襲也給市場增添了更多不確定性，推動油價上漲。Timera Energy分析師在一份報告中寫道，“歐洲天然氣市場在庫存異常偏低的情況下進入冬季，應對進一步供應或需求衝擊的餘地有限。歐洲正在透過推高價格，與亞洲爭奪LNG供應。”截至阿姆斯特丹時間上午11:50，歐洲天然氣基準荷蘭近月期貨上漲2.4%，至每兆瓦時75.10歐元。

### 2026-09-08 17:55:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mongolia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Mongolia Inflation Rate Eases in August — The annual inflation rate in Mongolia eased to 12.5% in August 2026, following a more than three-year high of 13.0% in the previous month. Price growth moderated for food and non-alcoholic beverages (21.5% vs 25.5% in July), health, medical care and services (7.1% vs 7.5%), and miscellaneous goods and services (9.4% vs 9.8%). Meanwhile, inflation picked up for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (14.2% vs 12.6%), transport (11.2% vs 10.0%), education (17.4% vs 12.8%), clothing, footwear, and cloth (7.4% vs 7.2%), furnishing and household equipment (8.9% vs 8.7%), recreation and culture (7.7% vs 6.6%), housing and utilities (5.4% vs 5.1%), restaurants and hotels (16.4% vs 16.2%), and insurance and financial services (17.1% vs 17.0%). At the same time, prices remained steady for communication at 1.6%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.1% in August, up from 0.9% in the previous month.

【HSBC: US equities not expensive; valuations don’t fully reflect AI upside】HSBC global CIO Willem Sels said US equities are not as expensive as they appear and valuations still do not fully reflect an AI-driven productivity and earnings upswing. He noted the US–Europe P/E gap has narrowed but multiples have yet to price an AI-led structural investment cycle; semiconductor names are particularly discounted and skepticism over 2027 earnings forecasts should fade as companies provide concrete order flow and guidance. Sels remains broadly bullish, saying markets have repeatedly shrugged off shocks because economic and corporate resilience has outpaced expectations and governments and firms have acted proactively. He added that AI adopters show stronger revenue, earnings and margin growth—especially in the US—signaling tangible productivity gains. The main market risk is a sharp rise in bond yields; he flagged the 10-year US Treasury around 5% as a potential trigger for volatility and warned markets have become complacent about low bond volatility.

【Japan and Iran foreign ministers hold 20-minute call】Japan foreign minister MOTEGI and Iranian foreign minister Araghchi held a 20-minute phone call at 16:30 Beijing time on Sept. 8, the Japanese foreign ministry said. MOTEGI voiced serious concern over a renewed escalation in U.S.-Iran military tensions, urged urgent de-escalation and a resumption of U.S.-Iran dialogue, and pressed Iran to show maximum flexibility. Araghchi outlined Iran’s position.

### 2026-09-08 17:27:32  #國際

【加拿大貿易反制精準施壓美國 加拿大對美國貿易反制底牌】加拿大東部時間今天（8日）零點，也就是北京時間今天（8日）12點，加拿大政府對美國商品加徵反制關稅的措施正式生效。加方表示，本次受反制關稅影響的美國商品總額與美方對加方加徵的50%關稅涉及商品價值相當，實現“等額、對等”反制。專家分析認為，加拿大“正在展示其能夠在貿易談判中與美國抗衡”。加拿大的策略是增加美國國內的壓力，最終迫使美方重返談判，但美國貿易保護主義不會發生重大改變。北京師範大學教授、經濟學專家萬喆表示，加方現在反制關稅規模和美方是完全對等的，覆蓋的是鋼鋁、乳製品、農業、家電、電子產品、消費品等。核心策略就是把關稅成本想要精準地傳導到美國政治敏感的地帶，試圖透過中期選舉的壓力，倒逼美方妥協。另外一張牌，是資源底牌，這也是加拿大最具威懾力的壓艙石的籌碼。包括原油、天然氣，鉀肥等。 當然，它還有一張牌是產業鏈繫結，像美加製造業，尤其是汽車產業，是高度一體化的，跨境生產協同非常緊密，對於加拿大加徵關稅本質上也是在對美國本土來加稅，可能就會影響美國車企的成本、還有汽車大州的就業等等。（央視財經）

Croatia Trade Deficit Widens in July — Croatia’s trade deficit widened to EUR 1.8 billion in July 2026 from EUR 1.7 billion in the same month a year earlier. Exports increased 2.8% year-on-year to EUR 2.3 billion, while imports rose 4.5% to EUR 4.1 billion. During January-July, exports grew 9.8% to EUR 16.0 billion, supported by higher shipments to both EU (12.6%) and non-EU countries (4.1%). Meanwhile, imports rose 6.9% to EUR 28.6 billion, driven by stronger purchases from both the EU (5.6%) and non-EU countries (11.7%). Over the same period, the trade gap widened slightly to EUR 12.6 billion from EUR 12.1 billion.

【布倫特原油升向100美元關口 襲擊導致沙特暫停部分能源設施執行 (1)】布倫特原油逼近每桶100美元，此前沙烏地阿拉伯表示，該國南部數個能源設施遭到襲擊後已暫停運營。這一全球基準油價距離每桶100美元大關已不到1美元。中東敵對行動再度升級推動油價上漲。沙特官方通訊社沙特新聞社表示，襲擊發生在週二，造成多人受傷。伊朗支援的胡塞武裝稱，再次襲擊了沙特日產40萬桶的吉贊煉油廠以及服務國內市場的設施。為回應利雅得方面對葉門首都薩那的圍困，胡塞武裝正在封鎖沙特石油運輸，並在最近幾周多次發動襲擊。針對這個石油輸出國組織最大產油國的襲擊行動，進一步加劇了市場對石油供應持續受擾的擔憂。胡塞武裝的襲擊以及伊朗戰爭均對供應構成威脅。全球石油庫存正在迅速下降，柴油等成品油價格則在世界各地飆升。今年迄今，布倫特原油已上漲逾60%，歐洲柴油基準價格也正逼近每桶200美元。高盛集團分析師Daan Struyven等在一份報告中表示，“市場越來越多地在消化中東衝突長期持續的情境。”該行基於航運中斷將持續至2027年的假設，小幅上調了油價預測。“我們價格預測面臨的風險仍明顯偏向上行。”

### 2026-09-08 17:15:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Iceland #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Iceland Trade Gap Widens to 4-Month High — Iceland’s trade deficit widened to ISK 63.1 billion in August 2026 from ISK 33.6 billion in the same month a year earlier, marking the largest deficit in four months. Imports surged by 35% year-on-year to ISK 135.5 billion, driven largely by increased purchases of fuels and lubricants (+74%), transport equipment (+63%), industrial supplies (+31%), consumer goods (+7%), and food and beverages (+5%). Meanwhile, exports rose by 8% to ISK 72.4 billion, lifted by higher shipments of farmed fish (+52%), manufactured products (+33%), which offset declines in agricultural products (-53%), marine products (-14%), and other products (-75%). Over the last twelve months, Iceland’s trade deficit totaled ISK 470.0 billion, moderating from ISK 501.6 billion a year earlier. Exports recorded no growth at ISK 954.8 billion, while imports dropped by 2% to ISK 1,424.8 billion.

### 2026-09-08 17:14:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 2

South Africa Economy Contracts More than Expected — South Africa's economy shrank by 0.2% over a quarter in Q2 2026, following a downwardly revised 0.4% expansion in Q1 and more than the expected 0.1% decline. This marked the first economic downturn since Q3 2024, snapping its longest quarterly growth run in almost a decade, amid the fallout from the Middle East conflict. Three of the ten industries contracted, with trade, catering and accommodation industry down 1.9%, while energy-intensive manufacturing and mining fell 1.8% and 3.0%, respectively. Agriculture, however, provided some cushion, rising 0.3% on stronger activity in horticulture and field crops. Growth was also recorded in utilities (1%); general government services (1%); transport, storage and communication (0.9%); personal services (0.6%) and construction (0.4%). On a yearly basis, the GDP rose by 0.9% in Q2, marking a sharp slowdown from 1.9% in Q1 and missing the 1% growth expected by markets.

### 2026-09-08 17:06:26  #宏觀 #公司

【中國建設科技有限公司原副總經理徐文龍被開除黨籍】中央紀委國家監委網站9月8日通報，據中央紀委國家監委駐國務院國資委紀檢監察組、四川省紀委監委訊息：日前，經中央紀委國家監委批准，中央紀委國家監委駐國務院國資委紀檢監察組和四川省自貢市監委對中國建設科技有限公司原黨委委員、副總經理徐文龍嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，徐文龍喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對抗組織審查，搞迷信活動；違反組織原則，不如實報告個人有關事項，不如實向組織說明問題，違規為他人謀取人事方面利益並收受財物；廉潔底線失守，違規收受禮品、禮金，違規接受宴請，違規從事有償中介活動，違規兼職取酬，違規經商辦企業，違規從事營利活動；公器私用，違規干預和插手建設工程專案承發包活動；利用職務便利為他人在專案承攬、職務調整等方面謀取利益，並非法收受鉅額財物。徐文龍嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律和工作紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重、影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經國務院國資委黨委研究，決定給予徐文龍開除黨籍處分；經中央紀委國家監委駐國務院國資委紀檢監察組研究，決定按規定取消其享受的待遇；收繳其違紀違法所得；四川省自貢市監委將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

【Saxo警告稱日元走強或引發全球股市擁擠交易平倉】Saxo Markets認為，日元兌美元升值，可能引發全球股市部分擁擠且高槓杆頭寸的平倉。Saxo駐新加坡首席投資策略師Charu Chanana指出，從估值高企的軟體股、人工智慧相關半導體股票，到房地產投資信託基金(REITs)等利率敏感型股票，都面臨潛在風險。她在一份報告中寫道：“最脆弱的領域往往是高估值、持倉擁擠、高槓杆和流動性不足這幾個因素同時存在的地方。”Chanana補充稱：“多年來，投資者一直以極低成本借入日元，再將資金投向高收益貨幣和風險資產。面臨虧損或保證金要求上升的基金，可能首先拋售流動性最好、獲利最多的持倉，而不論這些公司的基本面是否發生變化。”

### 2026-09-08 16:50:58  #國際

【日本欲研發戰術AI衛星】據《日本經濟新聞》近日披露，日本防衛省有一項軍事太空新計劃，準備研發戰術AI衛星，為遠端導彈配備太空智慧偵察系統。根據公開的研發規劃，日本防衛省將聯合本土軍工企業，在2027年到2029年間研製戰術AI衛星的實驗機型，最快於2030財年進行太空在軌測試。目前，專案的研發經費已被列入日本2027年度防衛預算申請清單。總檯軍事評論員邵永靈指出，區別於傳統偵察衛星，戰術AI衛星可在軌完成情報分析，持續跟蹤時間敏感機動目標，簡短回傳結果報文還可降低被探測機率，大大提升了戰時韌性。而日本之所以突然急著要研製戰術AI衛星，是為了建立一套本國可控的天基快速情報鏈路，自主完成發現、識別、輸出座標，不再事事等待美國推送情報，減少被“卡脖子”的風險，在同盟內部爭取更大的作戰自主權。但這種急於求成的衛星一旦出現計算錯誤，則會引發誤判、誤操作，加劇局勢誤判風險。分析還指出，日本嘴上標榜“專守防衛”，實際卻搭建起完整的“發現、決策、打擊、評估”遠端殺傷鏈條，日本已然走上再軍事化道路，成為亞太地區和平穩定的最大威脅。（CCTV國際時訊）

【華源證券：維持國電電力“買入”評級，資產注入有望開啟中長期空間】華源證券研報指出，國電電力二季度業績穩健，資產注入有望開啟中長期空間。2026H1實現歸母淨利潤30.14億元，同比下降18.25%；其中2026Q2實現歸母淨利潤16.16億元，同比下降13.9%，業績下降主要受上網電價下行、燃料成本小幅上漲影響。分板塊來看，2026H1公司火電、水電、風電、光伏板塊歸母淨利潤分別為16.66、10.81、3.97、2.23億元，分別同比-15.31%、+22.42%、-24.95%、-62.27%。2026H1火電裝機同比增長超8%，電價下行煤價上漲影響火電利潤；2026H1雙江口水電站如期投產，水電盈利逆勢增長。公司擬現金收購集團資產近14GW，有望開啟中長期空間。當前股價對應的PE分別為16、13、12倍，按照0.22元/股的現金分紅，9月4日收盤價對應股息率為4.1%，維持“買入”評級。

### 2026-09-08 16:41:44  #其他

【全國衛星導航定位基準站網服務資料授權運營落地】9月4日，由自然資源部、國家資料局聯合主辦的全國衛星導航定位基準站網服務資料授權運營工作推進會在京舉行。會上宣佈，全國衛星導航定位基準站網服務資料授權運營落地。這是國家層面首次在時空資訊基礎設施領域開展公共資料授權運營，將推動基準站網公共資料從尚未充分釋放價值的公共資源轉化為可以合規流通使用的資料資產，標誌著北斗高精度時空資料要素邁入市場化運營新階段。 全國衛星導航定位基準站網是國家重要的空間資訊基礎設施。自然資源部統籌整合全國基準站資源，建成了法定權威、標準統一、精準可靠、安全可控的基準站“一張網”，搭建了“1+33”的北斗應用與基準服務中心協同運維管理體系，向社會提供國家統一的測繪基準服務，推進全國基準站統一規劃、統一標準、統一監管。 目前，“一張網”站點規模已擴充套件至7569座，其中國家站420座、省級站3293座、協同站3856座，為各行業提供高精度時空服務支撐，有力推動北斗規模化應用。 全國衛星導航定位基準站網資料是支撐數字中國建設和保障國家時空安全的重要資源，具有突出的公共服務屬性和商業開發潛力。據瞭解，全國衛星導航定位基準站網具備完整的資料採集、處理和分發能力，站網資料已於2025年在國家公共資料資源登記平臺完成登記。 此次授權運營是自然資源部落實“資料二十條”、公共資料“1+3”政策體系等的具體舉措。授權運營堅持安全可控、優質高效，兼顧市場需求與創新驅動，將構建覆蓋公益服務、重點行業、科研創新、產業發展的全域服務格局。授權運營將推行全流程安全合規管控與資料分類分級保護體系，嚴守資料安全底線。 推進會現場還進行了首批使用者意向簽約，簽約單位涵蓋氣象、地震、能源、交通等多個重要領域的十餘家行業機構和企業。（自然資源部）

Crude Oil Rises to Over 3-Month High — Crude rose to around $94.5 a barrel on Tuesday, the highest since June 3, after Saudi Arabia reported attacks that halted operations at several energy facilities in the south of the kingdom. The state-run Saudi Press Agency said the strikes wounded several people, while Iran-backed Houthi militants claimed responsibility for targeting the 400,000-barrel-a-day Jazan refinery and other facilities serving the domestic market. The attacks add to concerns over further disruption to global oil supplies amid ongoing Houthi strikes and the war involving Iran. Tensions are also rising around the Strait of Hormuz, where Iran said an agreement with Oman could soon provide a temporary safe-passage route, while warning that ships remain at risk of attack. Meanwhile, Chinese crude imports strengthened in August, with refiners increasing purchases from the Persian Gulf and other sources. Higher imports have enabled China to export more refined products, providing some relief to global oil markets.

Czech Republic Forex Reserves Hit Record High — Foreign exchange reserves in the Czech Republic rose to USD 184.6 billion at the end of August 2026 from USD 180.7 billion in the previous month. It marked the highest level on record, driven by higher foreign currency reserves, which climbed to USD 166.8 billion from USD 165.1 billion, while gold holdings rose to USD 12.6 billion from USD 11.0 billion. The country’s IMF reserve position increased slightly to USD 0.775 billion from USD 0.769 billion, while holdings of Special Drawing Rights (SDRs) edged up to USD 3.54 billion from USD 3.51 billion. Additionally, other reserve assets rose to USD 0.80 billion from USD 0.26 billion.

【沙特能源設施遇襲 布倫特原油逼近100美元年內漲幅超六成】沙烏地阿拉伯境內多處南部能源設施遭襲擊後停止運轉，布倫特原油逼近每桶100美元關口。受中東衝突升級、購油需求回暖推動，國際基準油價上週大幅拉昇，距離百元關口不足1美元。沙特官方通訊社表示，襲擊發生於週二，造成多人受傷。胡塞武裝稱，再次打擊沙特日處理能力40萬桶的吉贊煉油廠以及服務國內市場的多處能源設施。針對歐佩克最大產油國的持續襲擊，加劇市場擔憂：受胡塞襲擊以及伊朗局勢影響，原油供應或將持續遭遇擾動。全球原油庫存快速下降，全球範圍內柴油等成品油價格大幅飆升。今年以來布倫特原油漲幅已超60%，歐洲柴油基準價格逼近每桶200美元。

【油氣糧三重衝擊 全球“再通脹”擔憂升溫】全球持續數年的去通脹程序正在遭遇新的供給側衝擊。中東局勢持續擾動原油和天然氣供應，歐洲天然氣庫存處於多年同期低位；與此同時，不斷增強的厄爾尼諾現象開始威脅全球糧食生產。油、氣、糧三類基礎商品價格同時面臨上行風險，令市場對全球“再通脹”的擔憂重新升溫。摩根大通認為，真正值得關注的並非單一的糧食或能源衝擊，而是兩者可能產生疊加效應。其經濟學家測算，“超級厄爾尼諾”本身可能在峰值階段將全球食品通脹推高約0.7個百分點；若疊加中東局勢導致的柴油、化肥及包裝成本上漲，全球食品通脹受到的衝擊可能擴大至1.3至1.5個百分點，並使2027年全球總體通脹較原先預期高約0.3個百分點。（新華財經）

### 2026-09-08 16:19:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Bulgaria Retail Sales Growth Hits Six-Month Low — Retail sales in Bulgaria rose 5.6% year-on-year in July 2026, easing from a 7.1% increase in June and marking the slowest growth since January 2026. Activity eased for non-food products excluding fuel (4.1% vs 9.9%), with growth slowing in textiles, clothing, and footwear (14.7% vs 22.5%), mail-order and internet retail (22.5% vs. 26.4%), and computer and communications equipment (1.9% vs 8.0%). Meanwhile, activity accelerated for food, beverages, and tobacco (7.5% vs 4.1%) and automotive fuel in specialized stores (6.6% vs 3.1%). Growth in pharmaceutical and medical goods, cosmetics, and toiletries also edged up (1.5% vs 1.0%), while sales of food, beverages, and tobacco in specialized stores improved (-0.2% vs -1.0%). On a monthly basis, retail sales fell 0.2% in July, reversing a 0.5% increase in June.

### 2026-09-08 16:18:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Taiwan Inflation Rate at 4-Month Low — Taiwan’s annual inflation rate slowed to 2.04% in August 2026 from 2.54% in the previous month. While this was the lowest reading since April, it still marked the fourth consecutive month of inflation above the central bank’s 2% target, keeping investors on edge and reinforcing expectations of a rate hike ahead of the Central Bank of the Republic of China’s policy decision next week. Food prices eased sharply to 0.79% from 2.49% in July, marking the lowest increase since deflation in March. Inflation also moderated for housing (2.31% vs 2.32%), health (0.61% vs 0.76%), and miscellaneous goods and services (2.73% vs 3.27%), while education and entertainment remained stable at 3.22%. In contrast, prices accelerated for transportation and communication (2.77% vs 2.72%) and clothing and footwear (2.09% vs 1.66%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.13%, easing from a revised 0.19% in July and hitting their lowest level since November 2025.

Slovakia Trade Balance Shifts to Deficit in July — Slovakia posted a trade deficit of EUR 0.8 million in July 2026, shifting from a surplus of EUR 216.5 million in the corresponding month of the previous year and missing market expectations of a EUR 96.2 million surplus. This marked the first deficit since January, as imports outpaced exports. Year-on-year, imports climbed 9.4% to EUR 9,062.8 million, mainly due to higher purchases of machinery and transport equipment, which increased by nearly 11%. Imports from the EU rose almost 13%, while those from non-EU countries increased by approximately 4%. Meanwhile, exports rose 6.6% to EUR 9,062.0 million, driven largely by a more than 6% increase in shipments of machinery and transport equipment. Additionally, exports to the EU grew nearly 7%, while those to non-EU countries increased almost 7%. Over the January–July 2026 period, the country’s trade surplus widened to EUR 1,655.1 million from EUR 1,458.4 million in the same period of 2025.

Austria Trade Deficit Narrows Sharply in June — Austria’s trade deficit narrowed sharply to EUR 29.2 million in June 2026 from EUR 747.0 million in the same month a year earlier. This marked the smallest trade deficit since the last surplus in February, as export growth outpaced imports. Exports jumped 15.4% year-on-year to EUR 17.7 million, lifted by increased shipments to both intra-EU and extra-EU countries, which rose 15.7% and 14.8%, respectively. Export growth was supported by machinery and vehicles (+17.2%) and chemical products (+21.8%). Import growth was strongest in fuels and energy (+24.9%), chemical products (+14.1%), and machinery and vehicles (+10.6%). In the first half of 2026, the trade balance recorded a deficit of EUR 3.7 million, as imports (+5.9%) rose faster than exports (+5.4%).

Output decreased in ten out of the twelve industry groups, with the largest decline in the transport sector, which sank 21.3%, after a 27.8% surge in June, followed by electronics manufacturing (-13.9% vs 25.6%) and the metal industry (-11.6% vs 11.7%). Denmark Trade Surplus Widens in July — Denmark’s seasonally adjusted trade surplus widened to DKK 40.5 billion in July 2026, from a downwardly revised DKK 25.3 billion in June, marking the largest surplus in the current series. Total exports rose 4.0% month-on-month to DKK 202.7 billion, with goods exports increasing 4.5% to DKK 118.9 billion and services exports rising 3.3% to DKK 83.8 billion. Meanwhile, imports fell 4.4% to DKK 162.2 billion, driven by a 6.9% decline in goods imports, while services imports decreased 1.4%. In the first seven months of 2026, total exports reached DKK 1,313.0 billion, up from DKK 1,236.5 billion a year earlier, while imports rose to DKK 1,113.2 billion from DKK 1,045.8 billion with Denmark recorded a trade surplus of DKK 199.8 billion, up from DKK 190.7 billion in the same period of 2025.

### 2026-09-08 14:21:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Lithuania #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Lithuania Inflation Rate Hits 3-Year High — Lithuania’s annual inflation rate rose to 6.1% year-on-year in August 2026 from 5.9% in the previous month, marking the highest reading since August 2023. The increase was driven by faster price growth for transport (15% vs 12.5% in July) and housing and utilities (15.8% vs 15.1%). Costs also increased for recreation, sport, and culture (6.2% vs 5.2%), personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods and services (6.9% vs 5.7%), furnishings, household equipment and maintenance (2.8% vs 2.4%), restaurants and hotels (5.9% vs 5.5%), and information and communication (2.1% vs 1.6%). Meanwhile, inflation eased for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (7.1% vs 7.6%), clothing and footwear (0.8% vs 1.8%), and education (5.2% vs 5.3%), while it held steady for health (4.9%). Deflation also persisted in food and non-alcoholic beverages (-2.0% vs -0.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell 0.1% in August, reversing a 0.2% increase in the previous month.

### 2026-09-08 14:17:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Current Account #Importance 1

Denmark Current Account Surplus Hits Record High — Denmark’s seasonally adjusted current account surplus widened to DKK 44.4 billion in July 2026 from a downwardly revised DKK 29.0 billion in the previous month. This marked the largest increase on record, as the goods account surplus rose to DKK 33.4 billion from DKK 22.0 billion, lifted by a 4.5% rise in exports, while imports fell by 6.9%. Moreover, the services account surplus also increased to DKK 7.1 billion from DKK 3.3 billion, while the secondary income deficit narrowed to DKK 3.0 billion from DKK 3.6 billion a month earlier. On the other hand, the primary income surplus decreased to DKK 6.8 billion from DKK 7.3 billion. In the January–July period, the current account surplus stood at DKK 208.5 billion, up slightly from DKK 208.4 billion in the same period a year ago.

【ASML pushes next-generation lithography upgrade to target large data-center chips】ASML said it will work with key customers to develop new solutions for equipment capable of producing the most advanced chips to meet demand from designers of large data-center processors such as NVIDIA. Current widely used extreme-ultraviolet (EUV) scanners can manufacture dies up to about 800 mm2. ASML's next-generation high-numerical-aperture (High-NA) EUV machines deliver finer resolution but are constrained in maximum die area by smaller reticles. ASML plans to demonstrate a pilot line using larger reticles by 2031 and to have the technology ready for high-volume production by 2033.

【CPCA: High oil prices accelerate fuel-to-electric shift; NEV penetration reaches record high】CPCA said August 2026 China passenger-car market showed five market-driving features: overall volumes recovered m/m but remained weak y/y with extreme structural divergence as fuel vehicles cool sharply and NEVs lead; sustained high oil prices are accelerating fuel-to-electric substitution. Fuel-vehicle retail fell more than 40% y/y in August, nearly halving; non-plug-in hybrids were relatively resilient. NEV retail was 1.005 mln units in August (y/y -10.1%, m/m +5.7%), lifting NEV penetration to a record 65.2%. Exports continued to underpin wholesale and capacity: manufacturers’ wholesale was 2.353 mln units in August (y/y -5.3%, m/m +4.5%), with wholesale y/y performance 18.3 percentage points stronger than retail. Industry inventories kept falling; high oil prices are squeezing fuel-vehicle dealers, eroding dealer confidence and keeping channel pressures elevated amid weak domestic wholesale and retail.

【ECB likely to hike again this week but cautious on further action】The ECB is expected to raise its key rate on Thursday, marking a second increase since the outbreak of the US‑Iran war, but officials are likely to signal caution on further tightening as higher borrowing costs could curtail activity. Eurozone annual inflation rose to 3.3% in August, a near three‑year high and well above the ECB’s 2% target. Policymakers see no clear second‑round effects yet from the war‑linked closure of the Strait of Hormuz and the energy price spike, but warned prolonged conflict and sustained high energy prices would raise the risk of wage pressure and labour action, and that household heating costs should jump as winter approaches. President Lagarde is expected to stress that rates are not on a preset path and that further hikes are not inevitable, though she is unlikely to rule out additional action; some economists now see a third hike in December as possible.

【英特爾與阿斯麥推進高NA EUV量產，已處理超100萬片晶圓】9月8日，英特爾晶圓代工（Intel Foundry）與阿斯麥（ASML）宣佈，雙方正持續推進高數值孔徑（High NA）極紫外光刻（EUV）技術的量產應用。英特爾目前已透過早期裝置認證、研發及部分量產環節處理超過100萬片晶圓，高NA EUV已用於英特爾酷睿Ultra 3系列處理器“Panther Lake”部分層的量產。英特爾表示，採用高NA EUV的英特爾18A製程產品效能達到或超過使用傳統0.33數值孔徑EUV光刻平臺的同類層。雙方還將繼續推動光罩從目前的6英寸向6×12英寸大尺寸光罩過渡，同時透過光罩拼接技術，讓客戶在現有6英寸光罩條件下獲得高NA EUV的部分優勢。

### 2026-09-08 14:11:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Balance of Trade #Importance 2

German Trade Surplus Largest in Two Years — Germany’s trade surplus widened to €21.3 billion in July 2026 from €15.4 billion in June, exceeding expectations of a rise to €16 billion and marking the largest trade surplus since August 2024, as exports fell less sharply than imports. Exports went down 0.8% month-on-month to €138.2 billion, compared to expectations of a flat reading, as sales to EU countries declined 1.6%, while shipments to third countries edged up 0.2%, supported by a 19.1% jump in exports to the US. Exports to China fell 9.5%, while those to the UK declined 7.2%. Meanwhile, imports dropped 5.7% to €116.9 billion, as imports from EU countries fell 6.1%, while those from third countries decreased 5.3%, led by a 7.5% decline in imports from China.

### 2026-09-08 14:10:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Exports #Importance 1

German Export Drops 0.8% in July — Germany's exports decreased 0.8% month-on-month to €138.2 billion in July 2026, following a 0.9% increase in the previous month and compared with market expectations for no change. Shipments to EU member states declined 1.6% to €78 billion, mainly due to a 2.8% decrease in exports to the euro area (€53.9 billion), while exports to non-euro area countries increased 1.1% to €24.1 billion. Exports to third countries rose 0.2% to €60.2 billion, as stronger demand from the US (+19.1%) more than offset declines in exports to China (-9.5%) and the UK (-7.2%). For the January-July period, German exports increased 4% from the same period a year earlier, totaling €954.2 billion.

Australia 10Y Yield Moves Near 2011 Highs — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield traded around 5.2%, near its highest level since mid-2011 as markets increasingly priced in further tightening by the Reserve Bank amid persistent inflation pressures. RBA Assistant Governor Sarah Hunter said policymakers have limited tolerance for inflation remaining elevated and could raise rates for a fourth time this year if price pressures prove stronger than expected. Markets now see a 68% chance of a 25-basis-point hike to 4.60% at the September 29 meeting, up from below 10% a month ago, while the odds of rates reaching 4.85% next year have climbed to 60%. The hawkish repricing widened the 10-year yield’s premium over US Treasuries to 43 basis points, the most in three months. Still, fresh surveys pointed to growing pressure on the economy from higher borrowing costs, fuel prices and the weakening housing market. NAB’s business conditions index fell to a six-year low in August, while consumer sentiment weakened sharply in September.

Rupiah Firms on Dollar Weakness, Reserve Boost — The Indonesian rupiah strengthened below IDR 17,650 per U.S. dollar on Tuesday, reversing prior losses as broad dollar weakness supported the currency, while yen gains on expectations of more aggressive BoJ tightening and continued carry-trade unwinding reinforced the broader FX move. Domestically, sentiment was buoyed after Finance Minister Purbaya Yudhi Sadewa voiced confidence that GDP growth would remain solid in Q3 and Q4 despite recent natural disasters, stressing that the state budget faced no significant risks. Stronger external buffers also lent support, with August forex reserves hitting a five-month high. However, upside was capped by inflation concerns, as local media reported Bank Indonesia and the government stepped up joint market interventions to rein in volatile food prices, amid annual inflation of 3.19% in August. Investors also stayed cautious ahead of August consumer confidence data due Wednesday, followed by retail sales later in the week.

Aussie Dollar Hovers Near Multi-Month Highs — The Australian dollar hovered above $0.72, near multi-month highs as increasingly hawkish signals from the Reserve Bank boosted expectations for another rate hike this month. RBA Assistant Governor Sarah Hunter said the board has little tolerance for inflation remaining above target for an extended period and may need to raise interest rates further if price pressures prove stronger than expected. Markets now price around a 69% chance of a 25-basis-point hike to 4.60% at the September 29 meeting, up sharply from less than 10% a month ago, while a November move is fully priced in. The currency also benefited from broad US dollar weakness and extended gains against the euro, which slipped to a near two-year low. However, domestic headwinds limited further gains, with NAB business conditions falling to a six-year low in August and consumer sentiment declining sharply in September amid concerns over higher borrowing costs, fuel prices and the weakening housing market.

【First eight BSE three‑month holding‑period thematic funds approved; issuance imminent】China’s Beijing Stock Exchange (BSE) has approved the first eight actively managed, open‑end thematic funds with a three‑month holding‑period mechanism, clearing the way for near‑term issuance. Managers include Huaxia, Huitianfu, E Fund, Southern Asset Management, Harvest, East Money, Fullgoal and CITIC JianTou. The products target high‑quality, innovative small‑and‑mid‑caps on the BSE. Compared with earlier two‑year periodic open thematic funds, these require only a three‑month minimum holding before investors can redeem, materially shortening investor lock‑up and increasing allocation flexibility. Industry participants say the funds expand public mutual fund access to the BSE, should prompt fund managers to boost BSE research and product allocation, and may introduce incremental and institutional medium‑to‑long‑term capital that supports market liquidity.

【新設新質生產力專區 第二十六屆投洽會今天開幕】今天，第二十六屆中國國際投資貿易洽談會（簡稱“投洽會”）在廈門國際博覽中心正式開幕。本屆大會以“擴大雙向投資 共促全球發展”為永久主題，來自129個國家和地區的客商將在此展開為期四天的投資對接和專案洽談。作為“投資中國”的標誌性平臺，本屆投洽會圍繞“投資中國”“中國投資”“國際投資”三大板塊展開，首次設立芬蘭與沙烏地阿拉伯“雙主賓國”、廣東與青海“雙主賓省”。本屆投洽會金融展區面積與展商數量雙雙翻倍，CIPS（人民幣跨境支付系統）專區首次落地，新設新質生產力專區，聚焦人工智慧、低空經濟等前沿領域。本屆投洽會期間，將舉辦40多項重點投資促進活動，配套開展包括資本對接、專場路演在內的近100場活動。“雲上投洽會”平臺，匯聚招商專案、企業融資、產業資金等資源，透過數智賦能實現找專案更便利、找資本更高效。

【機構：提前購機帶動2026年智慧手機生產總量優於預期，上修全年產量至10.7億支】根據TrendForce集邦諮詢最新手機產業調查，2026年第二季全球智慧手機生產總量達2.75億支，較去年同期下降8%，下降幅度較原先預測收斂，整體表現優於市場預期。TrendForce集邦諮詢分析，本季表現優於預期主要由兩項因素帶動：其一，部分消費者擔憂儲存器漲價持續推升整機成本，促使2027年新機售價再一波調升，因而選擇提前購機；其二，部分品牌先前因儲存器成本上漲而過度下調生產計劃，如今隨市況回穩而進行回補調整。在上述效應帶動下，TrendForce集邦諮詢將2026年全年智慧手機生產總量預測由原先的10.5億支上修至10.7億支，年減幅度也由16%收斂至14%。

### 2026-09-08 13:42:19  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Ends 1.1% Lower, Falls for 2nd Session — New Zealand stocks dropped 150 points, or 1.1%, to close at 13,793 on Tuesday, down for the second straight session, mainly weighed down by materials, technology, energy, and utilities. Rising oil prices also pressured sentiment amid the escalating conflict in the Middle East, raising concerns over inflation and boosting bets that central banks would raise interest rates. Caution also prevailed ahead of China’s consumer and producer price data due on Wednesday, amid persistently weak domestic demand. However, a report from Moody’s ratings agency capped the fall, saying New Zealand’s economic recovery is underway but warning that several issues remain unresolved. Fresh data from China, New Zealand’s top trading partner, showed that China’s export growth remained robust in August. Among the biggest laggards were Gentrack Group (-5.1%), Mercury NZ (-2.5%), ANZ Group (-2.4%), Mainfreight (-2.3%), Fletcher Building (-2.3%), Channel Infrastructure (-1.4%), and Infratil (-1.1%).

### 2026-09-08 13:37:19  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #CSI300 #Impact bullish #SSE Composite

【GF Securities: Industry business conditions improving; top-tier brokers widen divergence】GF Securities says securities-sector activity is improving while profitability is increasingly concentrated among top firms. Forty-two listed brokerages reported combined operating revenue of 364.0 bln yuan in H1 2026, up 44.5% YoY, and net profit attributable to shareholders of 154.7 bln yuan, up 49.3% YoY. Q2 net profit alone was 94.0 bln yuan, up 82% YoY and 55% QoQ; quarterly credit impairment jumped 662% YoY to 4.3 bln yuan. By business line in H1, investment gains (incl. fair value) +48% YoY, brokerage +54%, net interest +52%, investment banking +24%, and asset-management net income +24%. Market turnover rose while commission rates continued to fall; product scale and mix improved and buy-side advisory accelerated transformation. Brokerage revenues for the 42 firms totaled 98.4 bln yuan, +54% YoY. New public fund issuance rose 27% YoY; 15 fund houses reported combined net profit of 11.9 bln yuan, +35% YoY. Private fund filings in the first seven months reached 709.4 bln yuan, +67% YoY. Asset-management revenue for the 42 firms was 27.3 bln yuan, +24% YoY, with clear dispersion in growth rates. Investment view: sector is undervalued and underweight positions retain defensive qualities with upside; corporate earnings recovery underpins a gradual market rebound, internationalisation offers a second growth vector, and easing industry policy could provide catalysts. GF notes Q3 may see market volatility and wider broker profit dispersion; greater exposure to tech-innovation increases earnings upside potential.

【Goldman: S&P correlation falls to 25-year low, index volatility may be underestimated】Goldman estimates the six‑month realized correlation among S&P 500 constituents has dropped to 11 — the lowest in about 25 years, with comparable readings only in Feb 2007 and Jan 2018. Low correlation reflects idiosyncratic stock moves tied to AI, earnings and sector dynamics that offset one another and suppress index realized volatility; Goldman cautions that a macro shock that re-synchronizes stock moves could rapidly amplify index volatility as diversification wanes. Cboe notes correlation spikes typically coincide with higher systemic risk and tail volatility: in March 2026 one‑month implied correlation rose from roughly 15 to 40, quickly pressuring dispersion trades.

Japan Services Sentiment Hits 6-Month High — Japan’s services sector sentiment index rose to 46.4 in August 2026 from 45.7 in the previous month, notching its highest level since February and slightly topping market expectations of 46.3. The latest reading marked the fourth consecutive month of improvement, lifted by stronger sentiment among household-related businesses, even as those in the food and beverage sector declined. Corporate confidence also improved, led mainly by the non-manufacturing sector, while labor market conditions continued to increase. Meanwhile, the economic outlook index, which measures business expectations for the months ahead, climbed to 48.3 from 45.8 in July, hitting a six-month high and rising for a fourth straight month. The improvement suggested businesses expect economic conditions to recover further despite concerns over the impact of natural disasters.

### 2026-09-08 12:59:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Netherlands #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Dutch August Inflation Confirmed at 3.3% — The annual inflation rate in the Netherlands edged up to a three-month high of 3.3% in August 2026 from 3.2% in July, confirming preliminary estimates. The pickup was mainly driven by higher inflation for housing and utilities (4.1% vs 3.9% in July), due to increased costs of electricity, gas and fuels. Inflation also rose further for alcoholic beverages (1.9% vs 1.6%) and clothing and footwear (1.1% vs 0.2%). Meanwhile, transport inflation eased slightly to 8.3% from 8.6%, while restaurants slowed to 3.8% from 4.6% and recreation, sports and culture to 2.2% from 3.2%. Food and non-alcoholic beverages prices fell further, declining 1% after a 0.4% drop in July. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.3%, easing from a 1.6% jump in the previous period. Meanwhile, HICP-based annual inflation, which excludes owner-occupied housing costs, slowed to 2.8% from 3% in July. Prices of goods and services purchased abroad also rose sharply, increasing 7.4% year-on-year from 4.8%.

Palm Oil Holds Gains Around MYR 5,000 — Malaysian palm oil futures extended their upward momentum, hovering around MYR?5,000 per tonne as a weaker ringgit and stronger edible oil prices in Dalian and Chicago lent support. Crude oil’s rally, driven by fears of prolonged Middle East conflict and supply risks, added further momentum. At the same time, an unusually severe El?Niño has deepened dry conditions across Southeast Asia, sparking fires and haze in Borneo and Sumatra, with more than 202,000 hectares reportedly burned, heightening concerns over crop yields. Yet gains were tempered by sluggish exports, with cargo surveyors estimating August shipments fell between 6.5% and 14.9% from July, while inventories climbed to a five-month high. In India, heavy vegetable-oil buying has congested major ports, delaying vessel unloading by up to 10 days as storage tanks overflow and refiners struggle to clear cargo, a bottleneck that could dampen near-term import demand.

### 2026-09-08 12:53:31  #市場

【四大底層邏輯共同驅動 資料拆解我國前8個月外貿成績單】海關總署今天對外公佈，今年前8個月，我國貨物貿易進出口同比增長17.6％，外貿執行延續兩位數快速增長態勢。亮眼的外貿成績單，不是短期偶然的脈衝，而是產業根基、市場佈局、經營主體、制度開放四大底層邏輯共同驅動的結果。底層邏輯一：完備產業鏈+新質生產力，供給端實現精準匹配全球新需求。外貿增長最硬核的底座，是全球最完整的製造業體系，疊加新質生產力持續釋放效能，實現“全球有什麼需求，中國就能快速供給”。底層邏輯二：市場多元化佈局，高訪外交打通經貿現實通道，分散外部波動風險。不再把增長押注在少數發達市場，依託高層外交、共建 “一帶一路”、上合等多邊合作機制，持續做大新興市場基本盤，實現“東方不亮西方亮”的貿易迴旋空間。底層邏輯三：民營經濟挑起大梁，各市場主體構成外貿活力源泉。外貿高增長的背後，是千萬家企業敢闖敢拼，民營企業作為第一大外貿主體，貢獻超56%的外貿增量。底層邏輯四：雙向開放的制度優勢，超大規模市場實現進出口雙向共振。今年來的外貿增長，不只是出口走強，進口增速持續高於出口增速，內外迴圈相互賦能，也是重要的底層邏輯。（央視）

【四川17條措施破解民企融資難融資貴：省內基金投資企業中，民企佔比不低於五成】從四川省委金融辦獲悉，該部門與省發展改革委、人民銀行四川省分行、四川金融監管局近日聯合印發《四川省金融支援民營企業發展的若干措施》，圍繞信貸、股權、債券、信用修復及融資對接等關鍵環節，出臺17條措施。《措施》明確，“十五五”時期全省民企貸款投放量持續擴大、貸款成本穩中有降，省內基金投資企業中民企佔比不低於五成。針對融資時效痛點，《措施》首次對金融機構辦貸時限劃出“硬槓槓”：原則上將線上貸款辦理時限壓縮至7日內、續貸3日內；線下貸款壓縮至15日內、續貸6日內。《措施》提出，建立“預授信白名單”，依託“川易貸”平臺精準推送至金融機構，並推動首貸、信用貸、無還本續貸、轉貸“四貸”聯動。

【香港證監會與金管局的2025年聯合調查：香港投資產品銷售創新高 固定收益、貨幣及商品相關的產品需求強勁】香港證券及期貨事務監察委員會與香港金融管理局（金管局）今天發表的年度聯合調查顯示，香港2025年非交易所買賣投資產品的銷售按年飆升63%，創下9.9萬億元的歷史新高。增長動力來自破紀錄的市場參與度及對固定收益、貨幣及商品相關的產品的強勁需求。參與客戶及公司的數目均創新高，推動總交易額強勁增長。完成至少一項非交易所買賣投資產品交易的客戶數目躍升33%至超過160萬名，而從事投資產品銷售的持牌法團及序號產生器構數目則上升9%至452家。值得注意的是，大型公司數目更上升27%至128家。集體投資計劃（增長85%）及結構性產品（增長53%）共同帶動銷售破紀錄，集體投資計劃更自2020年以來首次超越結構性產品，成為最暢銷的產品種類。

Copper Moves Toward Record High — Copper futures climbed to around $6.7 per pound on Tuesday, nearing fresh record highs as expectations of new US import tariffs on the metal continued to encourage traders to redirect shipments toward US warehouses, tightening supplies elsewhere. The persistent premium on copper futures traded on New York’s Comex continued to create arbitrage opportunities, even as the White House has yet to finalize the proposed levies. The tariff-driven trade triggered a major squeeze and backwardation on the LME last month, although new deliveries have helped ease some of the immediate supply pressure. Major copper-producing countries in South America have also faced operational challenges this year, contributing to weaker output and exports. Meanwhile, supply has struggled to keep pace with rising demand from data centers, renewable energy projects and power grids.

【SFC and HKMA 2025 joint survey: Hong Kong non-exchange-traded investment product sales jump 63% to HK$9.9 tln; fixed income, currency and commodity products dominate】The SFC and HKMA annual joint survey shows 2025 sales of non-exchange-traded investment products in Hong Kong rose 63% YoY to a record HK$9.9 tln. Growth was driven by record market participation and strong demand for fixed income, currency and commodity-related products; both client numbers and firm participation hit highs and total trading volumes rose sharply. Large distributors increased 27% to 128 firms. Collective investment schemes (+85%) and structured products (+53%) together pushed sales to the record level, with collective schemes overtaking structured products as the top-selling category for the first time since 2020. Investors continued to favor yield and liquidity-management solutions, making fixed income, currency and commodity-related products central to allocations. Among the top-five collective investment plans reported by large firms, money market funds accounted for 88% of sales (80% in 2024). Currency-linked product sales rose 50% YoY to HK$698 bln. Debt securities sales have continued a steady multi-year rise, up 43% vs 2022, led by sovereign bonds (+138%) and investment-grade corporate bonds (+43%).

Rupee Retreats on Oil, Importer Demand — The Indian rupee fell to around 94.61 per dollar, retreating from more than two-month highs as rising oil prices and increased importer hedging demand pressured the currency. The rupee’s recovery has lost momentum as companies lock in dollar requirements amid concerns over costlier crude. Brent crude neared $97 a barrel as escalating US-Iran tensions and threats to Gulf energy infrastructure raised supply disruption risks. Meanwhile, the RBI remained active after selling at least $8 billion last week, with estimates as high as $15 billion. Its role has shifted toward cushioning pressure from higher oil prices and US Treasury yields rather than driving further appreciation. Persistent depreciation concerns have kept hedging flows tilted toward dollars, with importers increasing forward purchases and exporters holding back dollar sales. Policy-driven dollar inflows have given the RBI greater room to intervene, while foreign exchange reserves recently hit a record $740.8 billion.

Deutsche Bank says voter anger over economic stagnation, energy costs and migration is reshaping local politics and raising pressure on the federal government. ### 2026-09-08 11:50:13  #市場 #央行 #國際

【國際金融機構認為AI熱潮掩蓋美國經濟風險上升趨勢】國際金融機構野村日前釋出研究報告說，人工智慧（AI）的大規模發展已成為美國經濟增長重要引擎，但也在多個方面給美國經濟帶來壓力。AI發展如果受挫，可能暴露美國日益上升的風險溢價。報告說，美國AI熱潮帶來的風險包括晶片和電力價格上漲推動通脹上升、晶片及技術裝置進口激增擴大貿易逆差、大型雲端計算企業發行大量債券推高美國國債收益率等。此外，因淨資本流入和AI推動的美國股市走高帶來異常資本收益，美國國際投資頭寸淨負債（即全球其他地區持有的美元資產）大幅增加。據野村分析師測算，美國國際投資頭寸淨負債佔所有淨債權國國際投資頭寸淨資產總和的比率已飆升至80%。報告指出，由於美國股市估值偏高且經濟基本面走弱，如果AI發展受挫，可能引發美國資本市場大幅調整。鑑於外國投資者對美股的敞口較大，以及AI生態系統內的槓桿和迴圈融資，該調整可能演變為“全球避險事件”。報告說，一些觀點認為，由於美國經濟規模龐大、資本市場深度和流動性強，以及美元作為全球儲備貨幣的地位，美元資產“無可替代”。但是，美元資產優勢地位所依賴的支柱正在弱化，外界對美國財政和貿易政策、安全保障政策以及美聯儲獨立性的信任均有所下降。報告指出，美國仍高度依賴外資流入以彌補持續擴大的財政和經常賬戶赤字，但規模巨大的雙赤字無法持續。不斷攀升的債務負擔如同“懸在頭上的定時炸彈”，相關壓力正在累積。報告認為，鑑於美國國際投資頭寸淨負債激增以及其他國家國際投資頭寸資產高度集中於美國，外國投資者適度去風險化將“足以引發美元貶值”。（央視新聞）

China 10Y Yield at Near 2-Month Low — China’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 1.67% on Tuesday, nearing its lowest level since mid-July, as robust trade data strengthened expectations that external demand will support economic growth. Exports surged 25.0% year-on-year to USD 401.44 billion in August 2026, accelerating from 23.9% growth in July, while imports jumped 28.2% to USD 282.4 billion, up from 27.5% in the previous month and marking an eighth consecutive month of double-digit growth. The trade surplus widened to USD 119.09 billion in August from USD 101.09 billion a year earlier, matching expectations. The solid trade performance comes as Washington intensifies pressure on Beijing to narrow trade imbalances ahead of a planned Trump-Xi meeting, while trade frictions between Beijing and Brussels are also intensifying ahead of an October deadline to address imbalances that EU leaders increasingly regard as a strategic concern.

Offshore Yuan Steady Near 2023 Peak — The offshore yuan held steady around 6.70 per dollar on Tuesday, remaining near its strongest level since January 2023, as strong trade data reinforced optimism over China’s external demand. Exports climbed 25.0% year-on-year to USD 401.44 billion in August 2026, accelerating from 23.9% growth in July, while imports surged 28.2% year-on-year to USD 282.4 billion, picking up from 27.5% in July and extending double-digit growth for an eighth straight month. As a result, China’s trade surplus widened to USD 119.09 billion in August from USD 101.09 billion a year earlier, matching forecasts. The robust trade figures came as Washington stepped up pressure on Beijing to address trade imbalances ahead of a planned Trump-Xi meeting, while the two countries remain under a fragile tariff truce set to expire in November. Trade tensions between Beijing and Brussels are also mounting ahead of an October deadline to address imbalances, which EU leaders increasingly view as a strategic concern.

【Japan labor minister says GPIF still weighing asset allocation change】Japan's Health, Labor and Welfare Minister Ken'ichiro Ueno said the Government Pension Investment Fund (GPIF) is still considering whether it needs to adjust its asset allocation. He said GPIF judged in March that adjustments were unnecessary but is continuing to study the issue and that the current investment environment has not materially diverged from the assumptions underpinning GPIF's portfolio. GPIF's policy target remains 25% in each of four asset classes: Japanese domestic bonds, overseas bonds, Japanese equities and overseas equities. Analysts say sharply higher Japanese government bond (JGB) yields in recent months leave scope for GPIF to raise its domestic bond target. GPIF's assets under management were about 318 tln yen (~$2.1 tln) at end-June; a 1 percentage-point allocation move would imply flows of more than 3 tln yen.

【第22屆亞洲地區海岸警備機構高官會高階別會議在浙江杭州開幕】9月8日上午，第22屆亞洲地區海岸警備機構高官會高階別會議在浙江杭州開幕。來自韓國、新加坡、越南等23個國家（地區）的海上執法機構以及聯合國毒品和犯罪問題辦公室、《亞洲地區反海盜及武裝劫船合作協定》資訊分享中心2個國際組織的124名代表參加本次會議。本屆會議是中國海警局首次主辦的泛亞太地區多邊國際會議。此次會議，各方將進一步圍繞預防和打擊海上非法活動、海上搜救、海洋生態環境保護、資訊共享、聯合演習及能力建設等領域深入研討。會議還將聚焦海上非傳統安全，圍繞“踐行全球安全倡議”和“海洋碳匯執法”兩大主題，開展專題研討與交流，旨在攜手各方推進地區和全球海洋治理，共同應對海上風險挑戰，推動構建海洋命運共同體。

### 2026-09-08 11:17:45  #市場 #央行 #國際

【東方匯理：10年期美債收益率或闖5% 全球股市正處於十字路口】東方匯理資產管理亞洲高階投資策略師姚遠表示，鑑於10年期債息已處於近期交易區間上限，若油價突破每桶100美元，美聯儲啟動進取的緊縮政策，或者美國財政恐慌加劇，不排除債息上漲至5%的可能。股市方面，姚遠認為，全球股市正處於十字路口，尋求短期方向。AI生態系統中面臨的風險增加，超大規模雲端服務商自由現金流急劇下降，資本支出更依賴資本市場融資，AI債務供應指數級增長。同時，主權債券發行競爭加劇及央行收緊貨幣政策預期令資本成本正在上升，故任何融資成本上升正在抑制人工智慧支出的跡像或打擊市場情緒，引發新一輪拋售。此外，AI變現進展緩慢加劇市場憂慮，亦面對中國開源架構搶佔市場的威脅。因此，即使仍然看好AI長期發展前景，但已不再是整體水漲船高，當前投資需要更多的紀律與耐心，建議在中、中、下游多元佈局。

Putin reportedly ordered a three-day pause in Russian attacks on Kyiv to coincide with the US delegation’s visit to Ukraine, but stressed that the two sides had not agreed to a broader ceasefire. President Putin is reportedly determined to bring the entire Donetsk region under Russian control within six months, while Ukraine resumed strikes on Russian oil refineries on Monday. The intensifying conflict has disrupted grain trade, as attacks on Black Sea terminals and export infrastructure hamper agricultural exports.

### 2026-09-08 10:59:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Imports YoY #Importance 1

China Import Growth Below Estimates — China’s imports jumped 28.2% year-on-year to USD 282.4 billion in August 2026, accelerating from a 27.5% increase in the prior month and extending double-digit growth for an eighth consecutive month. However, the latest reading fell short of market expectations for a 30% rise. In January–August, imports increased 27.0% from the same period in 2025 to USD 2.11 trillion. During the period, purchases rose for copper ore (33.5%), coal and lignite (19.8%), integrated circuits (61.7%), and high-tech products (46.0%). By source, imports grew from Japan (27.7%), Hong Kong (111.3%), the U.S. (3.4%), ASEAN (25.3%), and the EU (6.2%). In July, Chinese authorities pledged faster fiscal support and timely monetary easing but offered no concrete measures to boost household consumption. Meanwhile, the Beijing-Washington trade truce has held despite intermittent frictions, with both sides exploring reciprocal tariff cuts on USD 30 billion of goods as they prepare for another summit later in September.

【廣西橫州：嚴厲打擊假借氫能、甲醇、複合新能源燃料等名義建偽新能源加註站的違法行為】據南寧市橫州生態環境局訊息，為嚴厲打擊假借氫能、甲醇、複合新能源燃料等名義建設偽新能源加註站的違法行為，防範土壤、地下水汙染及安全生產風險，規範燃料經營市場秩序，9月3日，南寧市橫州生態環境局會同橫州市經信、自然資源、應急管理、公安、市場監管等部門，對轄區內偽新能源加註站開展專項聯合執法行動。針對排查發現的違法點位，各部門執法組依法分類處置：對存在環境違法行為的，依法下達責令改正文書，責令其立即停止加註經營，限期拆除加註裝置。下一步，南寧市橫州生態環境局將健全線索發現、聯合核查、線索移送等閉環工作模式，持續加大日常巡查和暗訪檢查力度，嚴防違法站點死灰復燃。同時，暢通群眾舉報渠道，廣泛宣傳偽新能源加註站的安全與環境危害，引導群眾共同參與監督，切實守護生態環境安全和群眾生命財產安全。

Japan 10Y Yield Slips as Yen Strengthens — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 2.89% on Tuesday, extending its retreat from 30-year highs as the yen extended gains to reach a seven-month high, easing inflationary pressures in the country. A stronger yen generally lowers the cost of imports, which can contribute to softer inflation. The currency’s rally was driven by the unwinding of carry trades, expectations of capital repatriation and growing US political pressure for Japan to support the yen through tighter monetary policy. Markets expect the Bank of Japan to raise interest rates this month, with an economic adviser to Prime Minister Sanae Takaichi saying the central bank is likely to hike rates in September and deliver another increase by January next year. Meanwhile, data showed Japanese wages rose at their fastest pace since 1997, while second-quarter GDP growth was revised higher, strengthening expectations for a more hawkish BOJ policy stance.

### 2026-09-08 10:49:45  #市場 #國際

【超強厄爾尼諾逼近 世界糧食計劃署：將加劇全球突發性糧食不安全問題】世界氣象組織確認厄爾尼諾現象已形成並將持續增強，預計將發展為超強級別事件。超強厄爾尼諾來襲，將對全球糧食安全造成怎麼樣的衝擊？聯合國世界糧食計劃署（WFP）在回應採訪時表示，厄爾尼諾現象本身或將推高受影響國家的糧食價格。農作物減產或歉收將導致當地糧食供應趨緊，進而推動食品價格上漲。與此同時，氣候衝擊往往會削弱就業和收入來源，降低家庭購買力。食品價格上漲與收入下降雙重壓力疊加，將使更多脆弱家庭難以獲得充足且富有營養的食物，從而進一步加劇突發性糧食不安全問題。最新分析顯示，在當前厄爾尼諾現象影響下，到2027年底，在全球45個本已面臨嚴峻糧食安全挑戰的國家和地區，預計將新增約4900萬突發性糧食不安全人口。屆時，這些國家和地區面臨突發性糧食不安全的人數或將從目前約2.25億人增至2.74億人，增幅約22%。其中，中美洲部分地區和南部非洲將成為受影響最嚴重的區域，東非以及南亞和東南亞也將受到顯著影響。世界糧食計劃署關注的不僅是厄爾尼諾現象帶來的乾旱、洪澇和極端高溫等直接影響，更關注這些氣候衝擊與貧困、衝突、經濟不穩定以及食品價格上漲等既有脆弱性因素相互疊加所造成的綜合影響。（人民財訊）

Meanwhile, steel exports rose 6.8% yoy to 10.16 million tons. European Gas Climbs Amid Supply Concerns — European natural gas prices rose to €73.7/MWh on Tuesday, hovering at their highest level in more than three and a half years, as LNG supply from the Persian Gulf remained severely disrupted amid heightened US-Iran tensions. The escalation in fighting between the two nations underscored the difficulty of reaching a resolution to the conflict that could reopen the Strait of Hormuz, with Iran on Monday warning that energy infrastructure across the region, including US oil and gas facilities, could be vulnerable to further attacks. Qatar has largely suspended LNG shipments and extended force majeure on cargoes bound for European and Asian markets through the autumn, as shipping disruptions continue along the critical waterway. The reduced inflow has slowed the pace of gas storage injections in Europe, with EU storage facilities around 66% full, below the seasonal average, leaving the gas market increasingly vulnerable as the heating season approaches.

【陝西6部門出臺意見推動小水電綠色轉型】從陝西省水利廳獲悉：該廳聯合省發展改革委、省自然資源廳、省生態環境廳等6部門印發《加快推動小水電綠色轉型高質量發展實施意見》。 《實施意見》明確，到2035年全省小水電佈局科學合理，生態保護水平大幅提升，生態流量全過程精準管控，河流連通性得到進一步改善，智慧化、集約化、標準化水平全面提升，基本建成符合陝西生態保護與高質量發展要求的現代小水電體系，小水電在農村供電保障、節能降碳、水風光儲協同、應急保供等方面作用有效發揮，形成與全省經濟社會發展全面綠色轉型相適配、與黃河流域生態保護和高質量發展相契合、滿足新型電力系統需求的小水電高質量發展格局。 《實施意見》以建設安全、綠色、智慧、惠民的現代化小水電為目標，從5個方面部署重點任務。一是嚴格控制開發建設，最佳化小水電佈局，嚴控新建專案，科學有序擴容改造，引導老舊電站規範有序退出。二是實施綠色改造提升，推動小水電提質增效，積極推進智慧化改造，加快構建集約化執行體系，推進標準化管理。三是強化生態保護修復，築牢河湖生態屏障，科學合理確定生態流量，嚴格落實生態流量洩放，推動河流連通性持續改善。四是壓實安全生產責任，提升安全保障水平，持續鞏固大壩安全，深入推進安全生產標準化建設，健全安全管理與應急體系。五是發揮綜合保障功能，助力鄉村振興與能源安全，深度助力鄉村全面振興，積極引導推進多能互補與系統融合，強化小水電應急保障能力。

【韓國第二季度名義GDP創47年來最快增速】韓國央行週二資料顯示，受強勁出口以及與人工智慧相關的投資推動，韓國第二季度名義國內生產總值創下47年來最快增速。根據韓國銀行的初步資料，該國實際國內生產總值（衡量經濟增長的關鍵指標）在4月至6月期間較三個月前增長0.6%。這一季度環比資料與韓國銀行上月釋出的先前預估值一致，但該行將建築投資和智慧財產權投資增速分別上調了0.1個百分點，同時將政府消費增速下調了0.1個百分點。作為亞洲第四大經濟體，韓國去年第四季度經濟萎縮0.1%，但第一季度反彈增長1.8%。同比來看，第二季度經濟擴張3.7%，延續了第一季度同比增長3.8%的增長勢頭。在半導體、機械和裝置全球需求強勁的支撐下，出口較上一季度增長1.3%。該增速較第一季度5.9%的增幅有所放緩。按名義價值計算，該國第二季度GDP較三個月前增長9.2%，較上年同期增長26.4%，創下自1979年第三季度（當時經濟同比增長27.7%）以來的最大增幅。央行資料還顯示，該國人均國民總收入名義值較上季度增長8.8%，較上年同期增長26.4%。實際國民總收入較上季度增長3.1%，較上年同期增長15.6%，創下自1988年第四季度（當時實際GNI同比增長15.7%）以來的最大增幅。

China Stocks Mixed as Trade Data Looms — The Shanghai Composite rose 0.3% to 3,945 on Tuesday, while the Shenzhen Component fell 0.3% to 13,731, delivering a mixed performance as investors adopted a cautious stance ahead of China's trade data due later in the day. Risk appetite was further tempered by renewed geopolitical frictions after Beijing announced preliminary anti-dumping measures on Japanese imports of a chemical used in semiconductor production. 【檢察機關依法辦理一批涉黑涉惡案件 推動深化掃黑除惡專項鬥爭起好步開好局】自7月深化掃黑除惡專項鬥爭開展以來，全國檢察機關依法辦理一批重大黑惡案件，共受理審查逮捕涉黑涉惡案件194件634人，批准逮捕104件342人；受理審查起訴涉黑涉惡案件275件1627人，起訴涉黑涉惡案件151件900人。據悉，今年7月中央政法委動員部署開展深化掃黑除惡專項鬥爭以來，全國檢察機關聞令而動、迅速部署、紮實推進，毫不動搖地堅持貫徹依法嚴懲方針，把打擊鋒芒始終對準人民群眾最痛恨、反映最強烈的各類黑惡勢力違法犯罪，最高檢和各省級檢察院組建1406名專業人才組成的掃黑除惡鬥爭人才庫，會同有關部門研究新型黑惡犯罪法律政策問答，開展專題培訓和崗位練兵，對苗頭性問題露頭就打，對新型黑惡勢力高壓嚴打，對潛藏蟄伏的黑惡勢力窮追猛打，決不讓黑惡勢力死灰復燃、坐大成勢。各級檢察機關在依法嚴懲傳統黑惡犯罪的基礎上，重點依法嚴厲打擊利用網路平臺和“軟暴力”手段有組織地實施裸聊敲詐、非法放貸、非法催收、輿情敲詐、惡意索賠、強迫消費、勞務碰瓷、色情敲詐等涉黑涉惡犯罪及相關犯罪；依法嚴厲打擊跟蹤滋擾、威脅恐嚇、敲詐勒索等“軟暴力”涉黑涉惡犯罪。據統計，在涉黑涉惡方面，全國檢察機關共批准逮捕非法催收犯罪17件63人，起訴10件25人；批准逮捕勞務碰瓷犯罪10件41人，起訴14件71人；批准逮捕裸聊敲詐犯罪11件23人，起訴2件19人；批准逮捕輿情敲詐犯罪8件33人，起訴2件8人。此外，以普通犯罪集團或團伙批准逮捕非法放貸、惡意索賠等犯罪195件492人，起訴67件195人。在辦理涉黑涉惡案件中，全國檢察機關充分發揮檢察偵查職能作用，依法查辦司法工作人員相關職務犯罪，對“關係網”“保護傘”一查到底、絕不姑息，依法立案偵查司法工作人員相關犯罪6件7人。（新華社）

【“十四五”智慧財產權成績單出爐 多項指標位居世界前列】第十五屆中國智慧財產權年會9月8日至9月9日在北京舉行，主題為“奮力推動‘十五五’智慧財產權事業高質量發展”。國家智慧財產權局介紹，“十四五”時期，我國智慧財產權工作從追求數量向提高質量轉變，智慧財產權事業實現新發展、新跨越、新提升。在法律制度方面，我國完成了專利法及其實施細則新一輪全面修改，建立了國際上最高標準的侵權懲罰性賠償制度。在創造方面，我國國內有效發明專利擁有量達到539.4萬件，國內有效商標註冊量達到5081.6萬件，PCT國際專利申請量連續7年位居全球第一，海牙體系外觀設計申請量、馬德里國際商標申請量均居世界前列。在審查方面，建成了國際領先的專利智慧審查系統和新一代商標審查系統，實現了多模態大模型在審查工作中的應用，專利商標審查週期均達到相同審查制度下國際最快水平。在運用方面，專利密集型產業增加值突破18萬億元，佔國內生產總值的比重提升到了13.38%。智慧財產權使用費年進出口總額超過4000億元。在保護方面，建成129家國家級智慧財產權保護中心和快速維權中心、116家海外智慧財產權糾紛應對指導工作平臺，智慧財產權保護社會滿意度提升至82.81分。在服務方面，實現了智慧財產權基礎資料應開放盡開放，各項公共服務“一窗通辦、一網通辦”，各類服務機構覆蓋更廣、服務更優。在國際合作方面，新加入《工業品外觀設計國際註冊海牙協定》和《馬拉喀什條約》，推動成功締結《產權組織智慧財產權、遺傳資源和相關傳統知識條約》和《利雅得外觀設計法條約》。世界智慧財產權組織釋出的《2025年全球創新指數報告》顯示，我國排名提升至第10位，首次躋身全球前十，擁有的全球百強創新叢集數量連續三年位居各國之首。

Hong Kong Stocks Extend Losses for Second Session — The Hang Seng Index fell 0.5%, or 130 points, to 25,283 on Tuesday, extending losses from the previous session as investor sentiment remained subdued amid concerns over rising oil prices and heightened geopolitical tensions. The decline came amid concerns that higher energy costs could fuel inflation and delay global interest-rate cuts. Technology stocks were among the major drags, with Tencent (-0.5%), Xiaomi (-0.9%) and Meituan (-0.3%) trading lower, while Hong Kong Exchanges and Clearing (-0.4%) also fell. Traders also grew cautious ahead of China’s August trade data due later today. Despite the broader weakness, Hong Kong's IPO market remained active. Semiconductor and memory-chip maker Shenzhen Longsys Electronics began trading on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange on Tuesday, after raising about HK$7.1 billion in its IPO. The listing highlights continued investor interest in China's technology and semiconductor sectors.

### 2026-09-08 09:54:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Private House Approvals #Importance 1

Australia Private House Approval Fall 4.2% — Private sector house approvals in Australia fell 4.2% month-on-month to 10,199 units in July 2026, confirming preliminary estimates and reversing a 0.8% increase in the previous month. This marked the first decline since April, reflecting weaker housing approval activity across all states. South Australia recorded the sharpest drop (-10.7%), followed by Queensland (-5.5%), Victoria (-4.1%), New South Wales (-4.0%), and Western Australia (-0.1%). Meanwhile, approvals for private-sector dwellings excluding houses slipped 0.4% to 7,119 units, pointing to softer momentum across the broader residential construction sector. On a yearly basis, private house approvals were 6% higher than in July 2025.

### 2026-09-08 09:51:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Australia Business Sentiment Slips to 3-Month Low — Australia’s NAB Business Confidence Index fell to -8 in August 2026 from -6 in the previous month, marking the lowest reading since May. Meanwhile, business conditions slid to a six-year low, dropping below the long-run average since mid-2025, amid declines in sales, profitability, and employment. The deterioration pointed to a broad-based weakening in business activity, with firms becoming more cautious about demand and operating conditions. The survey also suggested that cost pressures remained elevated, while forward orders softened, highlighting growing headwinds to business investment and hiring. The subdued confidence and weakening conditions may reinforce expectations that the Reserve Bank of Australia will remain cautious on further policy easing, particularly as inflationary pressures remain sticky.

### 2026-09-08 09:46:40  #MKT News #Copper #Impact bullish #S&P500 #DXY #Impact bearish

【Henan to boost housing consumption with purchase subsidies, loan interest subsidies】Henan’s housing and urban-rural development department and eight other provincial departments issued a policy package titled Several Measures to Coordinate Controlling Increment, Destocking, Improving Supply and Stabilizing the Real Estate Market, pledging stepped-up support for housing consumption including locally tailored purchase subsidies and loan interest subsidies. The package promotes real‑estate+ online and offline promotions tied to local culture and tourism to stimulate demand and backs diversified upgrade demand. It also relaxes unit-counting for qualification: buyers with no property within the administrative boundaries of the county (city, district) where the intended purchase is located may be treated as first-time buyers; multi-child households that already own property may have one unit deducted when counting owned homes for a subsequent purchase.

【河南：加快推進存量商品房收購工作】河南省住房城鄉建設廳、省發展改革委、省自然資源廳等9部門近日聯合印發《關於統籌推進控增量去庫存優供給著力穩定房地產市場的若干措施》。《若干措施》主要分為四部分共15條，透過加強住房供應管理、加大住房消費支援力度、支援多元化改善性住房需求、加大公積金支援力度，建設“好房子”等15條舉措，因城施策控增量、去庫存、優供給，有效滿足剛性和多樣化改善性住房需求，著力穩定全省房地產市場。加快推進存量商品房收購工作。統籌用好中央和省級城鎮保障性安居工程補助資金、保障性住房再貸款、地方政府專項債，支援收購存量商品房用作保障性住房、宿舍、人才房等。城中村改造、城市更新等專案中涉及房屋安置的，鼓勵收購存量商品房用作安置房。

### 2026-09-08 09:46:27  #其他

【河南：根據商品房庫存去化週期對房地產用地實行差異化管控】河南省住房和城鄉建設廳等九部門印發《關於統籌推進控增量去庫存優供給著力穩定房地產市場的若干措施》，其中提出，加強住房供應管理。堅持“以需定供”，根據城市發展、人口變化、住房需求等因素，科學編制“十五五”城鎮住房發展規劃，合理引導住房市場供應，實現以人定房、以房定地、以房定錢。強化房地聯動。根據商品房庫存去化週期對房地產用地實行差異化管控，對商品住宅庫存去化週期18個月以上的縣（市、區），暫停商品住宅用地出讓；12—18個月的，按照“盤活多少、供應多少”的原則，根據本年度內盤活的存量商品住宅用地面積（包括竣工和收回）確定新出讓商品住宅用地面積上限，確保不增加存量商品住宅用地面積。嚴格商品房預售管理。對分批預售但去化率較低的房地產專案，所在地住房城鄉建設主管部門應嚴格控制該專案後續商品房預售許可審批節奏。綜合考慮當前市場供求實際，進一步提高預售門檻。鼓勵實行現房銷售。

### 2026-09-08 09:44:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Building Permits MoM #Importance 1

Australia Building Approvals Confirm 3.6% Drop in July — Australia’s seasonally adjusted dwelling approvals fell 3.6% month-over-month to 17,687 units in July 2026, reversing a 6.9% increase in June, final estimates showed. The decline was driven by a 4.2% drop in approvals for private sector houses to 10,199 units, while approvals for private sector dwellings excluding houses edged down 0.4% to 7,119 units. Compared with a year earlier, total dwelling approvals increased 9.0%, with approvals for private sector houses rising 6.0% and those excluding houses climbing 19.9%. In trend terms, total dwelling approvals rose 0.8% to 18,365 units. Across the states, total dwelling approvals declined in Queensland (-13.9%), New South Wales (-8.1%), and Western Australia (-0.3%), while increasing in Tasmania (15.2%), Victoria (9.7%), and South Australia (5.9%). The value of total residential building fell 4.9% to AUD 11.26 billion, while the value of total non-residential building increased 14.4% to AUD 9.93 billion.

### 2026-09-08 09:39:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

South Korean Shares Hit 3-Week High — The benchmark KOSPI climbed more than 1% to around 7,095 on Tuesday, reaching a three-week high, as renewed optimism over AI-driven semiconductor demand continued to lift tech stocks. Samsung Electronics and SK hynix rose more than 1% and 3%, respectively. Expectations for stronger memory demand were reinforced by Samsung's 39.4% DRAM market share in Q2 and forecasts of critically tight supply as hyperscalers ramp up AI infrastructure spending. Other notable performers included SK Square (3.0%), Doosan Enerbility (3.3%), SK Inc. (1.2%), and Hanmi Semiconductor (4.8%). Additionally, South Korea's economic backdrop remained supportive, with August exports rising 68.7% year-on-year as semiconductor shipments surged more than 200%, while Q2 GDP expanded 0.6% quarter-on-quarter. Meanwhile, escalating Middle East tensions pushed Brent crude higher, raising inflation concerns and the risk of tighter global monetary policy.

### 2026-09-08 09:38:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Australian Stocks Trade Notably Lower — Australian shares slipped 55 points or 0.6% to 8,956 in early Tuesday trade, extending weakness from the prior muted session. Sentiment was pressured by a sharp fall in U.S. futures as Wall Street prepared for a shortened week against the backdrop of the Middle East war and rising trade frictions between Canada and the U.S. Locally, consumer confidence dropped in September for the first time since June, hit by higher fuel costs and renewed fears of further rate hikes. Traders also grew cautious ahead of China’s August trade data due later today. Losses were led by tech services, financials, retail, and transport, though gains in non-energy minerals and consumer durables helped cushion declines. The big four banks fell between 0.7% and 1.4%, while BHP Group (-0.6%), Fortescue (-1.6%), and Bluescope Steel (-5.5%) underperformed. In contrast, Greatland Resources rose 1.1% and AMP Ltd. added 0.6%.

【Liquidity gauge: market rates ease】Sept. 8 morning DR007 fell to 1.3719%. The PBOC conducted a 1 bln yuan 7‑day reverse repo, fully meeting primary dealers' demand. DR007 is a short-term money-market rate and is treated as China’s short‑end rate anchor; the spread between DR007 and the PBOC 7‑day reverse‑repo (policy) rate signals funding tightness—DR007 materially above the policy rate implies money‑market stress and may prompt PBOC liquidity injection, while DR007 at or below the policy rate indicates ample funding.
